Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the microevolution space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 54 and the convergence rate is O(176/n^2).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_54(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 54. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^176(S, F) is trivial. QED.
